# Tier 0 oracle stability and fits (phase0.7_tier0-stability-fits)

Task `phase0.7_tier0-stability-fits` (child of `phase0.8_tier0-goldens`, D3/D5/D9/D13). Numbers and hashes only: every recorded output stays in the owner's private store; the fitted formulas are published under `parity/semantics/`. Generated by `python -m tools.parity.fit run` (`parity/FITS.md`); do not edit by hand.

Comparison run: machine Bolado, Python 3.13.9, numpy 2.4.4, repository commit 4a3b56a plus the working tree of this task, Painter build of the oracles `12.1.5+js-api.1.1.20`, manifests `parity/envelopes/painter-12.1.5_js-api.1.1.20/manifests/tier0/`. Rules: `parity/CONTRACT.md` §2, §3.1-§3.2 (native decoded values; classification by measured storage format) and `14-parity-testing.md` §1, §5.

## 1. Summary

- Goldens compared: 164 (every Tier 0 manifest of the `manifests` above); stable 112, unstable 52.
- Exported maps compared: 9112 (unstable 1091); readbacks and captures compared: 9083 (unstable 39).
- `parity/semantics/ao-mixed.json`: 2 entries (exact 1, calibrated 1, gap 0).
- `parity/semantics/blend-modes.json`: 34 entries (exact 2, calibrated 31, gap 1).
- `parity/semantics/evaluator-profile.json`: 4 entries (exact 0, calibrated 4, gap 0).
- `parity/semantics/height-to-normal.json`: 14 entries (exact 10, calibrated 3, gap 1).
- `parity/semantics/resampling.json`: 4 entries (exact 1, calibrated 3, gap 0).
- `parity/semantics/srgb-transfer.json`: 3 entries (exact 1, calibrated 2, gap 0).
- 04 §9 items settled: 0 of 11 (section 6); every other item is an open item.
